# Get transactions from the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# First, calculate the start of the current week (Monday)
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Calculate 8 weeks ago from the start of current week
eight_weeks_ago = DATETIME(current_week_start, '-56 days')

# Filter transactions for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (date_time >= eight_weeks_ago) & 
    (date_time < current_week_start)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week start (Monday) for each transaction
    week_start = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    # Check if transaction occurred on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1 in DAYOFWEEK)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = recent_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.ASC())

WITH _t3 AS (
  SELECT
    sbtxdatetime AS date_time
  FROM main.sbtransaction
  WHERE
    sbtxdatetime < DATE(
      'now',
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
    AND sbtxdatetime >= DATETIME(
      DATE(
        'now',
        '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
        'start of day'
      ),
      '-56 day'
    )
), _s0 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT() AS agg_0,
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    ) AS week_start
  FROM _t3
  GROUP BY
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
), _s1 AS (
  SELECT
    SUM(IIF(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', date_time) AS INTEGER) IN (1, 7), 1, 0)) AS agg_1,
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    ) AS week_start
  FROM _t3
  GROUP BY
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
)
SELECT
  _s0.week_start,
  COALESCE(_s0.agg_0, 0) AS total_transactions,
  COALESCE(_s1.agg_1, 0) AS weekend_transactions
FROM _s0 AS _s0
LEFT JOIN _s1 AS _s1
  ON _s0.week_start = _s1.week_start
ORDER BY
  week_start

# Define the time boundaries for the analysis.
# The period covers the 8 weeks leading up to the current week.
# 'start of week' defaults to Monday, matching the requirement.
time_boundaries = Broker.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
)

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the specified 8-week period.
# Pre-calculate the start date of the week for each transaction and a flag to identify weekend transactions.
stock_transactions_in_period = time_boundaries.Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Partition the filtered transactions by the week's start date to group them.
# For each week, calculate the total number of transactions and the count of weekend transactions.
weekly_stock_summary = stock_transactions_in_period.PARTITION(
    name='weekly_groups',
    by=(week_start_date,)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start=week_start_date,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(Transactions.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.ASC())

result = weekly_stock_summary

# Get transactions from USA customers that occurred last week
usa_transactions_last_week = Customers.WHERE(
    LOWER(country) == LOWER("USA")
).transactions_made.WHERE(
    # Start of last week: current date, start of week, then go back 7 days
    (date_time >= DATETIME("now", "start of week", "-7 days")) &
    # End of last week: current date, start of week, then go back 1 day
    (date_time < DATETIME("now", "start of week"))
)

# Calculate the count and total amount

# Filter transactions for buy transactions in the past 10 days
recent_buy_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == LOWER('buy')) & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
)

# Group by ticker symbol and count transactions
ticker_buy_counts = recent_buy_transactions.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker.symbol
).PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=ticker_symbol).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker_symbol,
    num_buy_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 2 most frequently bought tickers
result = ticker_buy_counts.TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q8] Code generated by claude:
# Get customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# Calculate the date range: from 6 months ago to the start of current month
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months', 'start of month')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by join month to get customer signups per month
monthly_signups = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers)
)

# For PMAT, we need to calculate average transaction amount per month
# Get all transactions from these recent customers
customer_transactions = recent_customers.transactions_made.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(date_time), LPAD(MONTH(date_time), 2, '0')),
    transaction_amount = amount
)

# Partition transactions by month and calculate average
monthly_transactions = customer_transactions.PARTITION(name='tx_months', by=transaction_month).CALCULATE(
    month = transaction_month,
    pmat = AVG(transactions_made.amount)
)

# Combine the results - we need to join PMCS and PMAT by month
# Since we need both metrics for each month, let's use a different approach
# Calculate both metrics in one query by partitioning customers by join month

result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Then, create a new field 'type_of_ticker' to hold the ticker_type from the related Tickers collection.

    DATEDIFF("days", date, DATETIME('now')) <= 6
).CALCULATE(

# Step 1: Filter for customers who joined in 2022 and have transactions.
# A new field 'is_active_customer' is created to flag active customers (status is 'active').
customers_info = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    is_active_customer=IFF(LOWER(status) == 'active', 1, 0)
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered customers by country.
# For each country, calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) using the provided formula.
# The number of active customers is the sum of 'is_active_customer', and the total
# is the count of all customers in that group.
activity_ratio_by_country = customers_info.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    AR=(100.0 * SUM(customers_info.is_active_customer) / COUNT(customers_info))
)

).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    SPM=IFF(total_amount > 0, (total_amount - total_costs) / total_amount * 100.0, 0.0)
)

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    year = YEAR(date),
    month_num = MONTH(date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    year = year,
    month_num = month_num,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV to get previous month's average
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker"),
    momc = (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

# Step 1: Prepare the daily price data by extracting the symbol and creating a sortable month string (YYYY-MM).
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregate statistics.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="ticker_months", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly statistics by symbol to create groups for the window function.
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: For each ticker group, calculate the month-over-month change (MoMC) using the PREV window function.
# The PREV function looks at the previous month's average close within the same ticker group.
result = ticker_groups.ticker_months.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    average_closing_price,
    highest_price,
    lowest_price,
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_month_avg_close != None) & (prev_month_avg_close != 0),
        (average_closing_price - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close,
        None
    )
)

    customer_state = customer.state,

)

state_type_groups = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_combos", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(

)

# Filter transactions from the past 30 days (inclusive)
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 30
)

# Add country information to each transaction
transactions_with_country = recent_transactions.CALCULATE(
    country = customer.country,
    amount = amount
)

# Group by country and calculate aggregates
country_stats = transactions_with_country.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country = country,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    total_amount = SUM(Transactions.amount)
)

# Get top 5 countries by total transaction amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_amount.DESC())

# Get sales with sale price over $30,000 and their payments
high_value_sales_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    # Calculate the week start date (Monday) for each payment
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    # Determine if payment was on weekend (Saturday=6, Sunday=0 in some systems, but let's use DAYNAME)
    is_weekend=IFF(ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ('Saturday', 'Sunday')), 1, 0)
)

# Filter to only include payments from the last 8 weeks (excluding current week)
# Current week start would be DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
# We want to exclude current week and include 8 weeks before it
filtered_payments = high_value_sales_payments.WHERE(
    (week_start < DATETIME('now', 'start of week')) &
    (week_start >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks'))
)

# Group by week and calculate totals
result = filtered_payments.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(payment.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')

# Filter sales over $30,000, access their payments, and filter for the last 8 weeks.
# Also, calculate the week start date and a weekend flag for each payment.
payments_in_window = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_period) & (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=IFF(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date) >= 6, 1, 0)
)

    name="weekly_groups", by=(week_start_date,)
).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start_date,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(PaymentsReceived),

    # Car must have been sold

# Define a contextless expression to efficiently get the latest inventory snapshot for each car.
# This is done by ranking the snapshots by date in descending order for each car and picking the top one.
# SINGULAR() is used to treat the result as a single record, allowing direct field access.
latest_inventory_status = Cars.inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
).SINGULAR()

# Start with the Cars collection and filter for cars that meet two criteria:
# 1. The car has at least one sale record (HAS(sale_records) == 1).
# 2. The car's most recent inventory status is 'not in inventory' (is_in_inventory == False).
sold_and_out_of_stock_cars = Cars.WHERE(
    (HAS(sale_records) == 1) & (latest_inventory_status.is_in_inventory == False)
)

# Partition the filtered cars by make and model to group them.
# For each group, calculate the make, model, and the maximum sale price from all associated sale records.
# Finally, order the results by the highest sale price in descending order.
highest_sales_by_make_model = sold_and_out_of_stock_cars.PARTITION(
    name="car_groups", by=(make, model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=make,
    model=model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

# Get the date range for the last 6 months excluding current month
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
)

sales_data = eligible_salespersons.sales_made.CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson.salesperson_id,
    first_name=salesperson.first_name,
    last_name=salesperson.last_name,

    sale_price=sale_price,
    sale_date=sale_date
)

    (sale_date >= six_months_ago) & (sale_date < current_month_start)
)

    name="sp_month", 
    by=(salesperson_id, sale_month)

[DEBUG] [Q39] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the date range at the top level for access throughout the query.
# This establishes the start (6 months ago) and end (current month) of our window.
date_range_def = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Start with all sales and calculate the month of each sale. This creates a "scaffold"
# of all months that have at least one sale, ensuring we can report on months with
# zero sales for our specific salesperson group.
sales_with_month = date_range_def.Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month = DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

# Partition all sales by the calculated month. This groups all sales into monthly buckets.
monthly_groups = sales_with_month.PARTITION(name="monthly_sales", by=(sale_month))

# For each month group, calculate the required metrics.
# The filtering for salespersons (hired between 2022-2023) is applied *inside*
# the aggregation functions. This is the key to getting 0s for months that have
# sales, but not from the target salesperson group.
monthly_metrics = monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    month = sale_month,
    # PMSPS: Count sales where the salesperson was hired in the target years.
    PMSPS = COUNT(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    )),
    # PMSR: Sum sale_price for the same filtered group. Default to 0.0 if no such sales exist.
    PMSR = DEFAULT_TO(SUM(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    ).sale_price), 0.0)
)

# Finally, filter the aggregated monthly results to our desired time window (last 6 months)
# and order them chronologically.
pmsps_and_pmsr_metrics = monthly_metrics.WHERE(
    (month >= start_date) & (month < end_date)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, for each sale that has associated payments, we calculate the average number of days
# from the sale date to the payment date. This is done by calculating the day difference
# for each payment and then averaging those differences per sale.
per_sale_avg_days = Sales.WHERE(HAS(payment) == 1).CALCULATE(
    avg_days_to_pay_for_sale=AVG(
        payment.CALCULATE(
            day_diff=DATEDIFF("days", sale_date, payment_date)
        ).day_diff
    )
)

# Next, we calculate the overall average of these per-sale averages.
# This gives a single value representing the average payment time across all sales,
# which is then rounded to two decimal places as requested.
average_payment_time = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    average_days_to_payment=ROUND(AVG(per_sale_avg_days.avg_days_to_pay_for_sale), 2)
)

    payment_year = YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month = MONTH(payment_date)
)

    name="months", 
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = payment_year,
    month = payment_month,

    year = year,
    month = month,
    total_payments = total_payments,
    prev_month_payments = PREV(total_payments, by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()), default=0),
    mom_change = total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()), default=0)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

# Step 1: Extract year and month from each payment date.
payments_by_month = Dealership.PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month=MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Group payments by year and month, and calculate the total payment amount for each group.
# The result is ordered by year and month to prepare for the MoM calculation.
monthly_totals = payments_by_month.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC())

# Step 3: Calculate the Month-over-Month (MoM) change using the PREV window function.
# PREV retrieves the total_payments from the previous month based on the specified order.
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=total_payments,
    mom_change=total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC()))
)

# Calculate the start of the current week (Monday) and the previous week
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
previous_week_start = DATETIME(current_week_start, '-7 days')
previous_week_end = DATETIME(current_week_start, '-1 day')

# Filter payments from the previous week and group by date and payment method
payments_previous_week = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= previous_week_start) & 
    (payment_date <= previous_week_end)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    payment_amount=payment_amount
)

# Partition by date and payment method to get daily totals
result = payments_previous_week.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q60] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start and end dates for the previous week, assuming the week starts on Monday.
# 'start of week' gives the most recent Monday.
# '-7 days' from that gives the Monday of the previous week.
# '-1 day' from the start of the current week gives the Sunday of the previous week.
date_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_previous_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'),
    end_of_previous_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-1 day')
)

# Filter the PaymentsReceived collection to include only records within the calculated date range.
payments_in_last_week = date_boundaries.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_previous_week) & (payment_date <= end_of_previous_week)
)

# Partition the filtered payments by their date and payment method.
# Then, for each group, calculate the sum of the payment amounts.
daily_payment_summary = payments_in_last_week.PARTITION(
    name="daily_payments_by_method",
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_day=payment_date,
    method=payment_method,
    total_amount_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

# First, prepare sales data with year, quarter, and customer state
sales_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    sale_year = YEAR(sale_date),
    sale_quarter = IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, 1,
                      IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, 2,
                          IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, 3, 4))),
    customer_state = customer.state,
    sale_price = sale_price
)

# Partition by state and quarter to calculate totals
quarterly_sales = sales_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, sale_quarter)
).CALCULATE(
    state = customer_state,
    quarter = sale_quarter,
    quarter_start_date = IFF(sale_quarter == 1, "2023-01-01",
                            IFF(sale_quarter == 2, "2023-04-01",
                                IFF(sale_quarter == 3, "2023-07-01", "2023-10-01"))),
    total_sales = SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(state.ASC(), quarter.ASC())

result = quarterly_sales

sales_in_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023)

# Next, calculate the necessary fields for partitioning: the customer's state and the start date of the quarter for each sale.
# The quarter's start date is determined using nested IFF functions based on the month of the sale.
sales_with_quarter_info = sales_in_2023.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter_start_date=IFF(
        ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (1, 2, 3)),
        DATETIME('2023-01-01'),
        IFF(
            ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (4, 5, 6)),
            DATETIME('2023-04-01'),
            IFF(
                ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (7, 8, 9)),
                DATETIME('2023-07-01'),
                DATETIME('2023-10-01')
            )
        )
    )
)

# Partition the sales data by state and the calculated quarter start date.
# Then, for each group, calculate the total sales by summing the sale_price.
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_with_quarter_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups",
    by=(customer_state, quarter_start_date)
).CALCULATE(
    state=customer_state,
    quarter=quarter_start_date,
    total_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

# First, calculate the date range for the last 12 months excluding current month
# We'll filter treatments that started within this range
treatments_with_month = Treatments.WHERE(
    # Filter for treatments in the last 12 months, excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) > 0) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 12)
).CALCULATE(
    # Extract month in yyyy-mm format
    month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id=patient_id
)

# Partition by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC
result = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(name="months", by=month).CALCULATE(
    month=month,
    PMPD=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),  # Distinct patients diagnosed per month
    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)  # Total treatments per month
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the date boundaries for the last 12 full months, excluding the current month.
date_boundaries = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_12_months_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-12 months')
)

# Filter treatments to the specified period and create a formatted month string for grouping.
treatments_in_period = date_boundaries.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_12_months_ago) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0"))
)

# Partition by the formatted month and calculate PMPD and PMTC for each month.
monthly_metrics = treatments_in_period.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=treatment_month).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    PMPD=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(treatment_month.ASC())

result = monthly_metrics

# Step 1: Get patients with treatments and find their first treatment year
patients_with_first_treatment = Patients.WHERE(
    HAS(treatments_received) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    patient_id,
    first_treatment_year = YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group by year to count new patients per year
new_patients_by_year = patients_with_first_treatment.PARTITION(
    name="years", 
    by=first_treatment_year
).CALCULATE(
    year = first_treatment_year,
    number_of_new_patients = COUNT(Patients)
)

# Step 3: Calculate NPI (increase from previous year) using window functions
result = new_patients_by_year.CALCULATE(
    year,
    number_of_new_patients,
    npi = number_of_new_patients - PREV(
        number_of_new_patients, 
        by=year.ASC(), 
        default=0
    )
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC())

patient_first_treatment_year = DermTreatment.Patients.WHERE(
    HAS(treatments_received) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    first_treatment_year=YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group patients by their first treatment year and count them.
yearly_patient_counts = patient_first_treatment_year.PARTITION(
    name="year_groups", by=first_treatment_year
).CALCULATE(
    year=first_treatment_year,
    number_of_new_patients=COUNT(Patients)
)

# Step 3: Order by year and calculate the year-over-year increase (NPINPI).
npi_by_year = yearly_patient_counts.ORDER_BY(year.ASC()).CALCULATE(
    year=year,
    number_of_new_patients=number_of_new_patients,
    NPINPI=number_of_new_patients - PREV(number_of_new_patients, by=year.ASC())
)

# First, let's get treatments with their start year/month and count matching adverse events
treatments_with_matching_ae = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    start_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    start_month=MONTH(start_dt),
    matching_ae_count=COUNT(adverse_events.WHERE(
        (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt)) & 
        (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt))
    ))
)

# Now group by drug and sum up all matching adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_matching_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_matching_ae=SUM(treatments_with_matching_ae.matching_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_matching_ae
).TOP_K(1, by=num_adverse_events.DESC())

recent_treatments = Treatments.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 6
).CALCULATE(
    doctor_specialty=doctor.speciality,
    drug_amount=tot_drug_amt
)

# Group by specialty and calculate metrics

    name="specialties", 
    by=doctor_specialty
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doctor_specialty,
    num_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
)

# Get top 3 specialties by total drug amount
result = specialty_stats.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# First, filter treatments to include only those started in the last 6 months
# and calculate the doctor's specialty, which will be used for grouping.
recent_treatments_with_specialty = DermTreatment.Treatments.WHERE(
    start_dt >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months')
).CALCULATE(
    doc_specialty=doctor.speciality
)

# Next, partition the filtered treatments by the doctor's specialty.
# For each specialty, calculate the total number of treatments and the sum of the total drug amount.
# Finally, select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount in descending order.
top_specialties_by_drug_amount = recent_treatments_with_specialty.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_groups", by=(doc_specialty)
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doc_specialty,
    number_of_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# First, create a collection of male patients with private insurance and calculate their current age in years.
male_private_patients_with_age = DermTreatment.Patients.WHERE(
    (LOWER(gender) == 'male') & (LOWER(ins_type) == 'private')
).CALCULATE(

    avg_patient_age=ROUND(AVG(male_private_patients_with_age.age), 0)

merchant_cpur = Ewallet.Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    # First, we isolate the successful transactions for each merchant.
    # A transaction is successful if its status is 'success'.
    successful_transactions=transactions_received.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    # Now, we calculate the CPUR using the successful transactions.
    # We use IFF to handle cases where a merchant has no successful transactions, preventing division by zero.
    CPUR=IFF(
        NDISTINCT(successful_transactions.txid) > 0,
        NDISTINCT(successful_transactions.coupon_id) / NDISTINCT(successful_transactions.txid),
        0.0
    )
)

# Get notifications from US and Canada users for the last 3 weeks excluding current week
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to start of week
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    # Check if notification was sent on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(created_at), (1, 7))
)

# Partition by week and calculate totals
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.CALCULATE(
        weekend_flag = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)
    ).weekend_flag)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())

# Define the time boundaries for the analysis: the start of the current week and the start of the 3-week period prior.
time_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those from users in the US or Canada and within the specified time frame.
# Also, calculate the start of the week for each notification and a flag indicating if it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = time_boundaries.Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) & (created_at < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Group the filtered notifications by the week they were sent.
# For each week, count the total number of notifications and the number of weekend notifications.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Assign the final result to the output variable.
result = weekly_notification_summary

# First, we create a collection of user average balances (AMB) for the specified period.
# We filter the UserBalances to include only records from the last 7 days.
# Then, we partition these records by user_id to group them for each user.
# For each user's group of balances, we calculate their average balance (AMB).
user_ambs = Ewallet.UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
).PARTITION(name="user_groups", by=(user_id)).CALCULATE(
    amb=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Finally, we calculate the average of all the individual user AMBs calculated in the previous step.
# This gives the overall average AMB for users active in the last week.
average_balance_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_balance=AVG(user_ambs.amb)
)

# Get transactions from the last 2 months excluding current month
# First, calculate the date range
two_months_ago = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions and calculate month
transaction_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) & 
    (created_at >= two_months_ago) & 
    (created_at < current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    sender_id=sender_id,
    month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(created_at), LPAD(MONTH(created_at), 2, "0"))
)

# Partition by month and count distinct senders
result = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=month
).CALCULATE(
    month=month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Define the time boundaries for the last two full months.
time_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
)

# Step 2: Filter transactions to the specified period and sender type, and extract date components.
transactions_in_period = time_boundaries.Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) &
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_year=YEAR(created_at),
    transaction_month=MONTH(created_at),
    transaction_day=DAY(created_at)
)

# Step 3: Group by day to calculate the Daily Active Users (DAU).
daily_active_user_counts = transactions_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups",
    by=(transaction_year, transaction_month, transaction_day)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_year,
    transaction_month,
    dau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
)

# Step 4: Group the daily counts by month to calculate the average DAU (PMDAU) for each month.
# The month is formatted as 'yyyy-mm'.
pmdau_by_month = daily_active_user_counts.CALCULATE(
    month_yyyy_mm=JOIN_STRINGS('-', transaction_year, LPAD(transaction_month, 2, '0'))
).PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(month_yyyy_mm)
).CALCULATE(
    month=month_yyyy_mm,
    pmdau=AVG(daily_active_user_counts.dau)
)

result = pmdau_by_month

prev_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month')) &
    (created_at < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))

# Calculate STR

    str_percentage=100.0 * COUNT(prev_month_transactions.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')) / COUNT(prev_month_transactions)
)

# Define the time window for the previous month
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_previous_month = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-1 month')

# Isolate all transactions that occurred within the previous month
transactions_in_last_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the STR by dividing successful transactions by the total number of transactions
str_previous_month = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month)
).CALCULATE(
    str_rate=IFF(total_tx_count > 0, successful_tx_count / total_tx_count, 0.0)
)

# First, let's get users with their country and filter for those who sent transactions with sender_type = 0
users_with_transactions = Users.WHERE(
    HAS(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0)) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    user_country = country,
    user_id = uid,
    total_sent = SUM(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0).amount)
)

# Now partition by country to aggregate the data
country_stats = users_with_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=user_country
).CALCULATE(
    country = user_country,
    num_distinct_users = COUNT(Users),
    total_transaction_amount = SUM(Users.total_sent)
)

# Get the top 5 countries by total transaction amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

    total_number_of_transactions > 0
).TOP_K(

# First, identify all snapshots on the earliest date in 2023.
# This is done by filtering for the year 2023, then ranking the snapshots by date in ascending order.
# Using RANKING with allow_ties=True ensures that all snapshots from the same earliest date are selected.
# We then filter for rank 1 to get only the earliest snapshots.
earliest_snapshots_in_2023 = Ewallet.UserSettingSnapshots.WHERE(
    YEAR(snapshot_date) == 2023
).CALCULATE(
    rank_by_date=RANKING(by=snapshot_date.ASC(), allow_ties=True)
).WHERE(rank_by_date == 1)

# Next, calculate the average daily and monthly transaction limits from this filtered set of snapshots.
# The final result will contain the two requested average values.
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    avg_tx_limit_daily=AVG(earliest_snapshots_in_2023.tx_limit_daily),
    avg_tx_limit_monthly=AVG(earliest_snapshots_in_2023.tx_limit_monthly)
)
